# Girls' Frontline story inventory

Snapshot 2026-10-20. 2,253 readable stories in 60 collections. This list also retains 1 unavailable entries. [All games](../content-inventory.md).

No imported dialogue voices.

English originals.

Prepared local library only. Fresh portable, installer and static web copies have no GFL1 source downloader.

Original titles are preserved. Verified alternate title languages appear where supplied. "None" under voice means no imported spoken dialogue clips; background music and effects can still be present. Movie soundtracks can contain music without spoken dialogue.

## Through the Looking-Glass

Event, collaboration. 22/22 stories readable. Collection ID: `gfl1-through-the-looking-glass`.

Mission story order from installed EN data. Triggered dialogue, alternate endings and scenes without a confirmed placement are listed separately. 3 additional source scenes have unresolved replay placement. 1 original backgrounds are unavailable in this installation. 1 original sound-effect cues remain unavailable. Browser transitions, framing and text styling are approximate. Dialogue voices are not imported.

## Coverage limits

- Only original Steam EN dialogue is published. The legacy zh-CN text object duplicates English as a renderer fallback and is not a Chinese translation.
- No dialogue voice language is available. Music and sound effects are not dialogue voices.
- Movies are not imported for GFL1. Browser transitions, framing, actor scaling and some effects approximate native behavior.
- Chapter 14 is not available in the installed replay registry or scripts. Coverage is the selected native replay/event/collaboration inventory, not every script in the archive.
- One source scene is unavailable: Maze Guess, Initial Inference · round, script -59-EXT-AR1.
- Current missing optional source media: TEAPARTYNIGHT, LAKE, 2022saros_Sky, BGM_Pause, BGM_UnPause, SpecialActivity, bossbattle_loop, m_va_spirit_potion, AVG_2023Slow_Heart_telescreen3, AVG_20Winter_Error, AVG_20Winter_Open_Light, AVG_Division_Camera, AVG_Division_Flare_Gun, AVG_The_Division_Grenade_Explosion. Missing URLs are omitted, so local asset existence does not erase these gaps.
- Scenes without confirmed replay placement, alternate endings and gameplay-triggered dialogue remain separately labeled, not inserted into an invented narrative timeline.
- Native rolling credits and some native visual effects have explicit reader notices. Valhalla uses drink outcome choices instead of ingredient mixing.
